/// cf_math_pkg: Constant Function Implementations of Mathematical Functions for HDL Elaboration
///
/// This package contains a collection of mathematical functions that are commonly used when defining
/// the value of constants in HDL code.  These functions are implemented as Verilog constants
/// functions.  Introduced in Verilog 2001 (IEEE Std 1364-2001), a constant function (§ 10.3.5) is a
/// function whose value can be evaluated at compile time or during elaboration.  A constant function
/// must be called with arguments that are constants.
package cf_math_pkg;

    /// Ceiled Division of Two Natural Numbers
    ///
    /// Returns the quotient of two natural numbers, rounded towards plus infinity.
    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);
        longint remainder;


        remainder = dividend;
        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin
            remainder = remainder - divisor;
        end
    endfunction

    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);
        begin
            n = n-1;
            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)
                n = n>>1;
        end
    endfunction
    /// Index width required to be able to represent up to `num_idx` indices as a binary
    /// encoded signal.
    /// Ensures that the minimum width if an index signal is `1`, regardless of parametrization.
    ///
    /// Sample usage in type definition:
    /// As parameter:
    ///   `parameter type idx_t = logic[cf_math_pkg::idx_width(NumIdx)-1:0]`
    /// As typedef:
    ///   `typedef logic [cf_math_pkg::idx_width(NumIdx)-1:0] idx_t`
    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);
        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;
    endfunction

endpackage

// full adder, the basic module that constitutes the 3:2 compressor
module fulladder(
    input logic x,y,z,
    output logic sum,carry
);
    assign sum = (x ^ y ) ^ z;
    assign carry = ((x ^ y) & z) | (x & y);
endmodule

// 5:3 counter, the basic module that constitutes the 4:2 compressor
module counter_5to3(
    input logic x1,x2,x3,x4,cin,
    output logic sum,carry,cout
);
    assign sum = x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4 ^ cin;
    assign cout = (x1 ^ x2) & x3 | ~(x1 ^ x2) & x1;
    assign carry = (x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & cin | ~(x1 ^ x2 ^ x3 ^ x4) & x4;
endmodule

// Compare to obtain the larger of two signed numbers
module comparator #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8
)(
    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,
    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,
    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o
);
    logic [1:0] sign_ab;
    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;
    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;

    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};
    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];
    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];
    
    always_comb begin
        case(sign_ab)
            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;
            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;
            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;
        endcase
    end
endmodule

/*
The radix-4 booth encoding is as follows:
    code | value | operation
    000  |  +0   |     0
    001  |  +1   |    1*A
    010  |  +1   |    1*A
    011  |  +2   |    2*A
    100  |  -2   |   -2*A
    101  |  -1   |   -1*A
    110  |  -1   |   -1*A
    111  |   0   |     0
*/
module booth_encoder(
    input [2:0] code,
    output neg,
    output zero,
    output one,
    output two
);
    // Only when code = 100/101/110, neg = 1
    assign neg = code[2]&(~code[0]) | code[2]&(~code[1]);
    assign zero = ~(|code) | &code;
    assign two = ~code[2]&code[1]&code[0] | code[2]&(~code[1])&(~code[0]);
    assign one = code[1]&(~code[0]) | (~code[1])&code[0];
endmodule

// Generate partial product according to radix-4 booth encoding result
module gen_product #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 16
)(
    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] multiplicand,
    input logic [2:0] code,
    output logic [WIDTH:0] partial_prod,
    output sign
);
    logic neg,zero,one,two;
    booth_encoder u_booth_encoder(
        .code(code),
        .neg(neg),
        .zero(zero),
        .one(one),
        .two(two)
    );

    logic [WIDTH:0] temp_prod;
    always_comb begin
        if(one) begin
            temp_prod = multiplicand;
        end
        else if(two) begin
            temp_prod = multiplicand << 1;
        end
        else begin
            temp_prod = '0;
        end
    end
    assign partial_prod = neg ? (~temp_prod) : temp_prod;
    assign sign = neg;
endmodule

// Generate partial products based on modified sign extension in booth multipliers
module gen_prods #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_A = 16,
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_B = 16,
    // do not change
    parameter int unsigned COUNT = (WIDTH_B+2)/2,       // count of partial products generated in unsigned multiplication
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_A + WIDTH_B  // bit-width of outputs
)(
    input logic [WIDTH_A-1:0] operand_a,
    input logic [WIDTH_B-1:0] operand_b,
    output [COUNT-1:0][WIDTH_O-1:0] partial_prods
);
    logic [WIDTH_B+2:0] multiplier;
    logic [COUNT-1:0][2:0] codes;
    logic [COUNT-1:0][WIDTH_A:0] temp_prods;
    logic [COUNT-1:0] signs;

    assign multiplier = {2'b00,operand_b,1'b0};
    assign codes[0] = multiplier[2:0];

    // Generate partial products
    gen_product #(
        .WIDTH(WIDTH_A)
    ) ua_gen_product(
        .multiplicand(operand_a),
        .code(codes[0]),
        .partial_prod(temp_prods[0]),
        .sign(signs[0])
    );
    assign partial_prods[0] = {~signs[0], signs[0], signs[0], temp_prods[0]};

    generate
        genvar i;
        for(i=1;i<COUNT-1;i++) begin
            assign codes[i] = multiplier[2*i+2:2*i];
            gen_product #(
                .WIDTH(WIDTH_A)
            ) ub_gen_product(
                .multiplicand(operand_a),
                .code(codes[i]),
                .partial_prod(temp_prods[i]),
                .sign(signs[i])
            );
            // modified sign extension in booth multipliers
            assign partial_prods[i] = {1'b1,~signs[i],temp_prods[i],1'b0,signs[i-1]} << (2*i-2);
        end
    endgenerate

    assign codes[COUNT-1] = multiplier[2*COUNT:2*COUNT-2];
    gen_product #(
        .WIDTH(WIDTH_A)
    ) uc_gen_product(
        .multiplicand(operand_a),
        .code(codes[COUNT-1]),
        .partial_prod(temp_prods[COUNT-1]),
        .sign(signs[COUNT-1])
    );
    assign partial_prods[COUNT-1] = {temp_prods[COUNT-1],1'b0,signs[COUNT-2]} << (2*COUNT-4);

endmodule

// 3:2 compressor, compressing 3 inputs into sum and carry
// `operands_i` is a flat vector: element k occupies bits [k*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I].
// (Flat rather than a 2-D packed array so yosys's Verilog front end can read it.)
module compressor_3to2 #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)
)(
    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o
);
    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;
    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;
    generate
        genvar i;
        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin
            fulladder u_fulladder(
                .x(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .y(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .z(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .sum(sum[i]),
                .carry(carry[i])
            );
        end
    endgenerate

    assign sum_o = sum;
    assign carry_o = {1'b0,carry,1'b0};
endmodule

// 4:2 compressor, compressing 4 inputs into sum and carry
// `operands_i` is a flat vector: element k occupies bits [k*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I].
// (Flat rather than a 2-D packed array so yosys's Verilog front end can read it.)
module compressor_4to2 #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,                             // bit-width of inputs
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(4)   // bit-width of outputs
)(
    input logic [4*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o
);
    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;
    logic [WIDTH_I:0] cin;
    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] cout;
    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;
    
    assign cin[0] = 1'b0;

    // Cascaded 5:3 counters according to input bit-width
    generate
        genvar i;
        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin
            counter_5to3 u_counter_5to3(
                .x1(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .x2(operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .x3(operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .x4(operands_i[3*WIDTH_I + i]),
                .cin(cin[i]),
                .sum(sum[i]),
                .carry(carry[i]),
                .cout(cout[i])
            );
            assign cin[i+1] = cout[i];
        end
    endgenerate

    logic [1:0] carry_temp;
    
    assign sum_o = sum;
    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1]+cin[WIDTH_I];
    assign carry_o = {carry_temp,carry[WIDTH_I-2:0],1'b0};
endmodule

// Recursive implementation of carry-save-adder (CSA) tree, to compress inputs with any size into sum and carry
// `operands_i` is a flat vector: element k occupies bits [k*WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I].
// (Flat rather than a 2-D packed array so yosys's Verilog front end can read it.)
module csa_tree #(
    parameter int unsigned N = 8,           // size of inputs
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,     // bit-width of inputs
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)   // bit-width of outputs
)(
    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o
);
    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;
    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;

    generate
        if (N==1) begin
            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];
            assign carry_o = '0;
        end
        else if(N==2) begin
            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];
            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];
        end
        else if(N==3) begin
            compressor_3to2 #(
                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),
                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)
            ) u_compressor_3to2(
                .operands_i(operands_i),
                .sum_o(sum_o),
                .carry_o(carry_o)
            );
        end
        else if(N==4) begin
            compressor_4to2 #(
                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),
                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)
            ) u_compressor_4to2(
                .operands_i(operands_i),
                .sum_o(sum_o),
                .carry_o(carry_o)
            );
        end
        else begin
            logic [N_A*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_A;
            logic [N_B*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_B;
            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_A;
            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_B;
            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_A;
            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_B;

            // Divide the inputs into two chunks
            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0           +: N_A*WIDTH_I];
            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*WIDTH_I +: N_B*WIDTH_I];

            csa_tree #(
                .N(N_A),
                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),
                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)
            ) ua_csa_tree(
                .operands_i(operands_i_A),
                .sum_o(sum_o_A),
                .carry_o(carry_o_A)
            );

            csa_tree #(
                .N(N_B),
                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I),
                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)
            ) ub_csa_tree(
                .operands_i(operands_i_B),
                .sum_o(sum_o_B),
                .carry_o(carry_o_B)
            );

            logic [4*WIDTH_O-1:0] operands_i_C;
            assign operands_i_C = {sum_o_A, carry_o_A, sum_o_B, carry_o_B};
            
            compressor_4to2 #(
                .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_O),
                .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)
            ) uc_compressor_4to2(
                .operands_i(operands_i_C),
                .sum_o(sum_o),
                .carry_o(carry_o)
            );
        end
    endgenerate
endmodule

// Recursive implementation of comparator tree, to obtain the maximum value among inputs with any size
//
// `operands_i` is a flat vector of N elements, each (WIDTH+1) bits wide, element i
// occupying bits [i*(WIDTH+1) +: WIDTH+1]. (Written flat rather than as a 2-D packed
// array so the yosys Verilog front end can read this file directly.)
module comp_tree #(
    parameter N = 4,        // size of inputs
    parameter WIDTH = 8     // bit-width of inputs
)(
    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,
    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o
);
    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;   // bits per element
    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;
    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;

    generate
        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf
            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];
        end

        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair
            comparator #(
                .WIDTH(WIDTH)
            ) u_comparator(
                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),
                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),
                .result_o(result_o)
            );
        end

        else begin : g_split
            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;
            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;
            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_A;
            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_B;

            // Divide the inputs into two chunks
            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];
            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];

            // Module recursion for operands_i_A
            comp_tree #(
                .N(N_A),
                .WIDTH(WIDTH)
            ) ua_comp_tree(
                .operands_i(operands_i_A),
                .result_o(result_o_A)
            );

            // Module recursion for operands_i_B
            comp_tree #(
                .N(N_B),
                .WIDTH(WIDTH)
            ) ub_comp_tree(
                .operands_i(operands_i_B),
                .result_o(result_o_B)
            );

            // Compare result_o_A and result_o_B
            comparator #(
                .WIDTH(WIDTH)
            ) uc_comparator(
                .operand_a(result_o_A),
                .operand_b(result_o_B),
                .result_o(result_o)
            );
        end
    endgenerate
endmodule

// Modified radix-4 booth wallace multiplier
module radix4_booth_multiplier #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_A = 16,                // bit-width of operand A
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_B = 16,                // bit-width of operand B
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_A + WIDTH_B  // bit-width of the multiplication result
)(
    input logic [WIDTH_A-1:0] operand_a,
    input logic [WIDTH_B-1:0] operand_b,
    // the partial products are compressed into sum_o and carry_o
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,
    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o
);
    // count of partial products generated in unsigned multiplication
    localparam int unsigned COUNT = (WIDTH_B+2)/2;
    
    // ---------------
    // Generate partial products
    // ---------------
    logic [COUNT-1:0][WIDTH_O-1:0] partial_prods;
    gen_prods #(
        .WIDTH_A(WIDTH_A),
        .WIDTH_B(WIDTH_B)
    ) u_gen_prods(
        .operand_a(operand_a),
        .operand_b(operand_b),
        .partial_prods(partial_prods)
    );

    // ---------------
    // Compress the partial products into sum and carry
    // ---------------
    csa_tree #(
        .N(COUNT),
        .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_O),
        .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)
    ) u_csa_tree(
        .operands_i(partial_prods),
        .sum_o(sum_o),
        .carry_o(carry_o)
    );
endmodule

// barrel shifter
module barrel_shifter #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,               // bit-width of input data
    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,         // bit-width of shift amount
    // MODE=0-->shift left; MODE=1-->shift right
    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0
)(
    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,
    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,
    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o
);
    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;

    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;

    generate
        genvar i;
        // Left Shift
        if(MODE==1'b0) begin
            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin
                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];
            end
            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;
        end
        // Left Right
        else begin
            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin
                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];
            end
            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;
        end
    endgenerate
endmodule

/// A trailing zero counter / leading zero counter.
/// Set MODE to 0 for trailing zero counter => cnt_o is the number of trailing zeros (from the LSB)
/// Set MODE to 1 for leading zero counter  => cnt_o is the number of leading zeros  (from the MSB)
/// If the input does not contain a zero, `empty_o` is asserted. Additionally `cnt_o` contains
/// the maximum number of zeros - 1. For example:
///   in_i = 000_0000, empty_o = 1, cnt_o = 6 (mode = 0)
///   in_i = 000_0001, empty_o = 0, cnt_o = 0 (mode = 0)
///   in_i = 000_1000, empty_o = 0, cnt_o = 3 (mode = 0)
/// Furthermore, this unit contains a more efficient implementation for Verilator (simulation only).
/// This speeds up simulation significantly.
module lzc #(
  /// The width of the input vector.
  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,
  /// Mode selection: 0 -> trailing zero, 1 -> leading zero
  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,
  /// Dependent parameter. Do **not** change!
  ///
  /// Width of the output signal with the zero count.
  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)
) (
  /// Input vector to be counted.
  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,
  /// Count of the leading / trailing zeros.
  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,
  /// Counter is empty: Asserted if all bits in in_i are zero.
  output logic                 empty_o
);

  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc

    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];
    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];

  end else begin : gen_lzc

    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);


    // index_lut / index_nodes are flat: entry e occupies [e*NumLevels +: NumLevels].
    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;
    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;
    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;

    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;

    // reverse vector if required
    always_comb begin : flip_vector
      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin
        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];
      end
    end

    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut
      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));
    end

    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels
      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level
        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level
          // if two successive indices are still in the vector...
          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce
            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];
            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)
              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :
                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];
          end
          // if only the first index is still in the vector...
          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base
            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];
            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];
          end
          // if index is out of range
          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range
            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;
            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;
          end
        end
      end else begin : g_not_last_level
        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level
          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =
              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];
          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)
            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :
              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];
        end
      end
    end

    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};
    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);

  end : gen_lzc

endmodule : lzc

// Mantissa normalization
module mantissa_norm #(
    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,
    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,
    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3     // digits before decimal point
)(
    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,
    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,
    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o
);
    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);
    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;
    logic lzc_zeroes;
    
    // leading zero count
    lzc #(
        .WIDTH(WIDTH),
        .MODE(1'b1)
    ) u_lzc(
        .in_i(operand_i),
        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),
        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)
    );

    // Exponent adjustment
    always_comb begin
        if(lzc_zeroes) begin
            exp_adjust = '0;
        end
        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin
            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;
        end
        else begin
            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);
        end
    end

    // Mantissa normalization
    barrel_shifter #(
        .WIDTH(WIDTH),
        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),
        .MODE(1'b0)
    ) u_barrel_shifter(
        .operand_i(operand_i),
        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),
        .result_o(result_o)
    );
endmodule

// Posit decoder
module posit_decoder #(
    parameter int unsigned n = 16,  // word size
    parameter int unsigned es = 1,  // exponent size
    //do not change
    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),
    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,       // not include sign bit
    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3      // not include implicit bit
)(
    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,
    output logic sign_o,
    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,  
    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o
);

    // ---------------
    // Perform two's complement if negative
    // ---------------
    logic sign;
    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;

    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];
    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];
    
    // ---------------
    // Leading Zero Count
    // ---------------
    logic regS;
    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;
    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;
    logic lzc_zeroes;       // lzc_zeroes = 1 if input is all 0s

    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];
    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;

    lzc #(
        .WIDTH(n-1),
        .MODE(1'b1)    // mode=1 means "counting leading zeroes"
    ) u_lzc(
        .in_i(lzc_operand),
        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),
        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)
    );

    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;       // number of consecutive identical bits in regime field
    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;     // bitwidth of regime field
    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;   // value represented by regime field

    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;
    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);
    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});

    // ---------------
    // Left shift regime field
    // ---------------
    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;

    barrel_shifter #(
        .WIDTH(n-1),
        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),
        .MODE(1'b0)
    ) u_barrel_shifter(
        .operand_i(operand_value),
        .shift_amount(regime_bits),
        .result_o(op_no_rg)
    );

    // ---------------
    // Extract sign
    // ---------------
    assign sign_o = sign;

    // ---------------
    // Extract valid exponent
    // ---------------
    logic [es:0] exp;
    if(es==0) begin
        assign exp = 0;
    end
    else begin
        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];
    end
    
    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;

    // ---------------
    // Extract mantissa
    // ---------------
    logic implicit_bit;
    
    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];
    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};
endmodule

// Posit encoder
module posit_encoder #(
    parameter int unsigned n = 16,
    parameter int unsigned es = 1,
    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),
    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,       // not include sign bit
    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3      // not include implicit bit
) (
    input logic sign_i,
    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,
    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,

    output logic [n-1:0] result_o
);
    // ---------------
    // Determine whether input is effective 0
    // ---------------
    logic input_not_zero;
    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];

    // ---------------
    // Compute regime_k and exp
    // ---------------
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;
    logic signed [es:0] exp;

    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];
    if(es==0) begin
        assign exp = 0;
    end
    else begin
        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];
    end

    // ---------------
    // initial regime field
    // ---------------
    logic sign_k;
    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;
    logic [n-2:0] regime;
    
    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];
    assign rg_const = 1;
    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;

    // ---------------
    // Compute regime bits
    // ---------------
    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;
    
    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);
    
    // ---------------
    // Combine {regime, Exp, Mantissa}
    // ---------------
    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;   // regime + exponent + mantissa
    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;

    if(es==0) begin
        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};
    end
    else begin
        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]}; 
    end

    // ---------------
    // Calculate amount of right shift
    // ---------------
    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;
    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);
    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;
    
    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));

    // ---------------
    // Right Shift
    // ---------------
    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;
    
    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;
    barrel_shifter #(
        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),
        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),
        .MODE(1'b1)
    ) u_barrel_shifter(
        .operand_i(value_before_shift),
        .shift_amount(shift_amount),
        .result_o(value_after_shift)
    );

    // ---------------
    // Compute abs_value and rounding_bits
    // ---------------
    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;
    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;

    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];

    // ---------------
    // Perform rounding (RNE mode is applied by default)
    // ---------------
    logic round_bit;
    logic sticky_bit;
    logic round_value;
    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;

    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];
    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];
    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);
    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;

    // ---------------
    // Output result
    // ---------------
    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;
    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};
    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;

endmodule

/*
PDPU equipped with a fine-grained 6-stage
*/

module pdpu_top_pipelined #(
    parameter int unsigned N = 16,                  // dot-product size
    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,                 // word size
    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,                // exponent size
    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,
    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,
    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14         // alignment width
)(
    input logic clk,
    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,        // input vector (Va)
    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,        // input vector (Vb)
    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,                      // previous output (acc)
    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o                 // output result (out)
);
    // ********************
    // Pipeline 0: Input Reg
    // ********************
    // group_path -name pipe0to1 -from {operands_a operands_b operands_c} -to {pipe1*}


    // ---------------
    // Posit Decoding
    // ---------------
    // the maximum exponent size (not include sign bit)
    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;
    // the maximum mantissa size (not include implicit bit)
    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;

    // Extract valid sign, exponent, and mantissa from input vectors
    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;
    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;
    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_norm_a, mants_norm_b;

    generate
        genvar i;
        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding
            posit_decoder #(
                .n(n_i),
                .es(es_i)
            )ua_posit_decoder(
                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),
                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),
                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),
                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])
            );

            posit_decoder #(
                .n(n_i),
                .es(es_i)
            )ub_posit_decoder(
                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),
                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),
                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),
                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])
            );
        end
    endgenerate


    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;
    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;

    // Extract valid sign, exponent, and mantissa from previous output (acc)
    logic sign_acc;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;
    
    posit_decoder #(
        .n(n_o),
        .es(es_o)
    )uc_posit_decoder(
        .operand_i(acc),
        .sign_o(sign_acc),
        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),
        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)
    );

    // ---------------
    // Sign Process
    // ---------------
    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;
    logic [N:0] signs;
    
    // Perform XOR to obtain the signs of the product of Va and Vb
    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;
    assign signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};

    // ---------------
    // Exponent Addition
    // ---------------
    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O);
    
    // obtain the exponents of the product of Va and Vb through signed addition
    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;
    generate
        genvar m;
        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin
            assign rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) + $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);
        end
    endgenerate

    // ********************
    // Pipeline 1
    // ********************
    // group_path -name pipe1to2 -from {pipe1*} -to {pipe2*}
    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;
    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;
    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;

    `FFNR(pipe1_signs,signs,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_c,rg_exp_c,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_a,mants_norm_a,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_b,mants_norm_b,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_acc,rg_exp_acc,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe1_mant_norm_acc,mant_norm_acc,clk)

    // ---------------
    // Mantissa Multiplication
    // ---------------
    localparam int unsigned MUL_WIDTH = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);
    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] mul_sum, mul_carry;

    // Mantissa multiplication is performed by a modified radix-4 booth multiplier
    generate
        genvar j;
        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin: multiplication
            radix4_booth_multiplier #(
                .WIDTH_A(MANT_WIDTH_I+1),
                .WIDTH_B(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)
            ) u_radix4_booth_multiplier(
                .operand_a(pipe1_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)]),
                .operand_b(pipe1_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)]),
                .sum_o(mul_sum[(j)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH]),
                .carry_o(mul_carry[(j)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH])
            );
        end
    endgenerate

    // ---------------
    // Exponent Compare
    // ---------------
    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;
    generate
        genvar u;
        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin
            assign rg_exp_items[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_rg_exp_c[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];
        end
    endgenerate
    assign rg_exp_items[(N)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);

    // Obtain the maximum exponent by a recursive comparator tree
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;
    comp_tree #(
        .N(N+1),
        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)
    ) u_comp_tree(
        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),
        .result_o(rg_exp_max)
    );

    // *******************
    // Pipeline 2
    // *******************
    // group_path -name pipe2to3 -from {pipe2*} -to {pipe3*}
    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;
    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_rg_exp_items;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;
    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] pipe2_mul_sum, pipe2_mul_carry;
    
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_mant_norm_acc;


    `FFNR(pipe2_signs,pipe1_signs,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_items,rg_exp_items,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_max,rg_exp_max,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_mul_sum,mul_sum,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe2_mul_carry,mul_carry,clk)
    
    `FFNR(pipe2_mant_norm_acc,pipe1_mant_norm_acc,clk)

    // ---------------
    // The final addition
    // ---------------
    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] mants_norm_c;
    generate
        genvar v;
        for(v=0;v<N;v++) begin
            assign mants_norm_c[(v)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] = pipe2_mul_sum[(v)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] + pipe2_mul_carry[(v)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH];
        end
    endgenerate

    // ---------------
    // Mantissa Alignment
    // ---------------
    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;
    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product_shifted;

    // the mantissa products of Va and Vb are kept in values of bitwidth `ALIGN_WIDTH` before alignment
    generate
        genvar k;
        if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MUL_WIDTH) begin: fixed_shift
            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin
                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_WIDTH);
            end
        end  
        else begin: fixed_shift
            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin
                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] >> (MUL_WIDTH-ALIGN_WIDTH);
            end
        end
    endgenerate

    if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+2) begin
        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe2_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);
    end
    else begin
        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe2_mant_norm_acc >> (MANT_WIDTH_O+2-ALIGN_WIDTH);
    end

    // Align the products according to the difference between the respective exponent and the maximum exponent
    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);
    logic [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_diff;
    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;
    generate
        genvar z,s;
        // compute the respective exponent difference
        for(z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin
            assign rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - pipe2_rg_exp_items[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);
        end
        // the maximum shift amount is ALIGN_WIDTH, since the bits exceeding ALIGN_WIDTH will be discarded directly.
        if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin
            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin
                assign shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = (|rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH : rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: SHIFT_WIDTH];
                barrel_shifter #(
                    .WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH),
                    .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),
                    .MODE(1'b1)
                ) u_barrel_shifter(
                    .operand_i(product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),
                    .shift_amount(shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH]),
                    .result_o(product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH])
                );
            end
        end

        else begin
            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin
                barrel_shifter #(
                    .WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH),
                    .SHIFT_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH+1),
                    .MODE(1'b1)
                ) u_barrel_shifter(
                    .operand_i(product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),
                    .shift_amount(rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),
                    .result_o(product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH])
                );
            end
        end
    endgenerate

    // ---------------
    // Mantissa accumulation in two's complement
    // ---------------
    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);
    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;

    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa, mantissa_comp;
    // Convert to two's complement
    generate
        genvar y;
        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin
            assign mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];
            assign mantissa_comp[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = pipe2_signs[y] ? (~mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)]+1) : mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)];
        end
    endgenerate

    // *******************
    // Pipeline 3
    // *******************
    // group_path -name pipe3to4 -from {pipe3*} -to {pipe4*}
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;
    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe3_mantissa_comp;

    `FFNR(pipe3_rg_exp_max,pipe2_rg_exp_max,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe3_mantissa_comp,mantissa_comp,clk)

    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] csa_sum,csa_carry;
    csa_tree #(
        .N(N+1),
        .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1),
        .WIDTH_O(SUM_WIDTH+1)
    ) u_csa_tree(
        .operands_i(pipe3_mantissa_comp),
        .sum_o(csa_sum),
        .carry_o(csa_carry)
    );

    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_result;
    // the final addition
    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry;


    // ********************
    // Pipeline 4
    // ********************
    // group_path -name pipe4to5 -from {pipe4*} -to {pipe5*}
    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] pipe4_sum_result;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;

    `FFNR(pipe4_sum_result,sum_result,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe4_rg_exp_max,pipe3_rg_exp_max,clk)


    logic final_sign;
    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;
    
    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];
    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe4_sum_result+1) : pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];

    // ---------------
    // Mantissa normalization and exponent adjustment
    // ---------------
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;
    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;

    mantissa_norm #(
        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),
        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),
        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)
    ) u_mantissa_norm(
        .operand_i(sum_c),
        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),
        .result_o(sum_norm)
    );
    // Note: is there any possibility of overflow?
    assign final_rg_exp = pipe4_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;

    // ---------------
    // Posit encoder and Rounding
    // ---------------
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;
    
    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin
        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;
        logic sticky_bit;
        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];
        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;
        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};
    end
    else begin
        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);
    end


    // ********************
    // Pipeline 5
    // ********************
    // group_path -name pipe5to6 -from {pipe5*} -to {result_o}
    logic pipe5_final_sign;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;

    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,final_sign,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)


    posit_encoder #(
        .n(n_o),
        .es(es_o),
        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),
        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)
    ) u_posit_encoder(
        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign),
        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),
        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant),
        .result_o(result_o)
    );


    // ********************
    // Pipeline 6: Output Reg
    // ********************
    // No group_path
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
Now I have the full file. The key optimization opportunity is in the pipe2→pipe3 stage. The critical path is:
1. `pipe2_rg_exp_max` → exponent difference → shift amount → barrel shifter → two's complement → `pipe3_mantissa_comp`

The key idea: **move the two's complement conversion before the barrel shifter** (or restructure so the barrel shifter doesn't depend on the exponent comparison). Actually, the two's complement happens after shifting, which makes sense since negation distributes over right shift (approximately - for two's complement, `~(x >> n) + 1` isn't exactly `(~x+1) >> n`). But we can use the trick: for sign-magnitude, we can apply the sign *after* the CSA tree instead of before it.

Let me think about a better approach. The critical path involves 17 barrel shifters driven by `rg_exp_max - rg_exp_items`. A simpler approach: instead of finding the max and shifting all others to match, we could compute the alignment shift from the *product* exponents directly, but that changes behavior.

Actually, the key optimization: **the two's complement conversion (`mantissa_comp`) can be done in parallel with or moved into the pipeline stage.** Let me move the exponent difference and shift computation to be registered, and do only the barrel shift + complement in the pipe2→pipe3 stage. But we can't add more pipeline stages (must be 5).

Let me try a different approach: move the `mants_norm_c` addition (sum+carry) into the previous stage, and compute the product alignment inputs before pipe2, so the pipe2→pipe3 stage only has the barrel shifter + complement.

Let me create the optimized design:
  -> apply_diff(filename='starting_point.v', diff='--- a/starting_point.v\n+++ b/starting_point.v\n@@ -1,3 +1,4 @@\n+// Working copy for optimization\n // PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- readable SystemVerilog baseline.\n //\n // Source: github.com/qleenju/PDPU (ISCAS 2023, Apache-2.0), pdpu_top_pipelined,\n')

   always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,  \n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n    \n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    \n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    \n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n    \n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]}; \n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    \n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    \n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\n\nendmodule\n\n// ============================================================\n// OPTIMIZED PIPELINED PDPU TOP\n// Key change: moved mants_norm_c (sum+carry addition) and product\n// alignment (fixed shift) to before pipe2 register, so the pipe2→pipe3\n// stage only has barrel shifter + two's complement conversion.\n// This breaks the critical path that went through exp_max → exp_diff →\n// shift_amount → barrel_shift → two's_complement → pipe3.\n// ============================================================\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_norm_a, mants_norm_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(\n                .n(n_i),\n                .es(es_i)\n            )ua_posit_decoder(\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n\n            posit_decoder #(\n                .n(n_i),\n                .es(es_i)\n            )ub_posit_decoder(\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n    \n    posit_decoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o)\n    )uc_posit_decoder(\n        .operand_i(acc),\n        .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc),\n        .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc)\n    );\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] signs;\n    \n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O);\n    \n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign   // ********************\n    // Pipeline 1\n    // ********************\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_c,rg_exp_c,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_a,mants_norm_a,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_b,mants_norm_b,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_acc,rg_exp_acc,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mant_norm_acc,mant_norm_acc,clk)\n\n    // ---------------\n    // Mantissa Multiplication\n    // ---------------\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_WIDTH = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);\n    logic .WIDTH_A(MANT_WIDTH_I+1),\n                .WIDTH_B(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)\n            ) u_radix4_booth_multiplier(\n                .operand_a(pipe1_mants_norm_a[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: .carry_o(mul_carry[(j)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---------------\n    // Exponent Compare\n    // ---------------\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;\n    generate\n        genvar u;\n        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_items[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_rg_exp_c[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign rg_exp_items[(N)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree(\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    // ---------------\n    // OPTIMIZATION: Compute mants_norm_c (sum+carry) and product (fixed shift)\n    // in the pipe1→pipe2 stage instead of pipe2→pipe3\n    // ---------------\n    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] mants_norm_c;\n    generate\n        genvar v;\n        for(v=0;v<N;v++) begin\n            assign mants_norm_c[(v)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] = mul_sum[(v)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] + mul_carry[(v)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n    generate\n        genvar k;\n        if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MUL_WIDTH) begin: fixed_shift\n            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin\n                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_WIDTH);\n            end\n        end  \n        else begin: fixed_shift\n            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin\n                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] >> (MUL_WIDTH-ALIGN_WIDTH);\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+2) begin\n        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe1_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe1_mant_norm_acc >> (MANT_WIDTH_O+2-ALIGN_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // *******************\n    // Pipeline 2  -- now registers product (already fixed-shifted) + exp data\n    // *******************\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_rg_exp_items;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe2_product;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe2_signs,pipe1_signs,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_items,rg_exp_items,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_max,rg_exp_max,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_product,product,clk)\n\n    // ---------------\n    // Mantissa Alignment (variable shift) + two's complement\n    // Now the critical path is: exp_max → exp_diff → shift → barrel_shift → complement\n    // But product is already registered, so the barrel shifter input comes from a register\n    // ---------------\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product_shifted;\n\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);\n    logic [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - pipe2_rg_exp_items[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n        end\n        if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin\n                assign shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = (|rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? (~mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)]+1) : mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // *******************\n    // Pipeline 3\n    // *******************\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe3_mantissa_comp;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe3_rg_exp_max,pipe2_rg_exp_max,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe3_mantissa_comp,mantissa_comp,clk)\n\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] csa_sum,csa_carry;\n    csa_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1),\n        .WIDTH_O(SUM_WIDTH+1)\n    ) u_csa_tree(\n        .operands_i(pipe3_mantissa_comp),\n        .sum_o(csa_sum),\n        .carry_o(csa_carry)\n    );\n\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_result;\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry;\n\n    // ********************\n    // Pipeline 4\n    // ********************\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] pipe4_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe4_sum_result,sum_result,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe4_rg_exp_max,pipe3_rg_exp_max,clk)\n\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n    \n    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe4_sum_result+1) : pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm(\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe4_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    \n    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        logic sticky_bit;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Pipeline 5\n    // ********************\n    logic pipe5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,final_sign,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)\n\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder(\n        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
I see the issue now. The `pipe3_signs` is registered from `pipe2_signs`, but the `mantissa_comp` is combinational from `pipe2_signs`. Then `pipe3_mantissa_comp` is registered from `mantissa_comp`. So both `pipe3_mantissa_comp` and `pipe3_signs` are one cycle behind `pipe2_signs`. The CSA tree uses `pipe3_mantissa_comp` and the correction uses `pipe3_signs`. They should be aligned since both are registered.

But the problem might be the carry propagation. The CSA tree has `csa_carry` with a left-shifted carry. The `+1` correction at bit 0 should be correct. Let me verify the issue more carefully.

Wait - the problem might be that the original code uses `~mantissa+1` where `mantissa` is `product_shifted` zero-extended to `SUM_WIDTH+1` bits. The two's complement of a zero-extended number is `~mantissa + 1`. The ones' complement is `~mantissa`. The correction is `+1` per lane at bit 0. But since lanes are at different positions in the flat vector, bit 0 of each lane's `(SUM_WIDTH+1)`-bit segment is at different positions in the flat vector. When the CSA tree sums them, the `+1` at bit 0 of lane `y` is at position `y*(SUM_WIDTH+1)` in the flat vector. But in the CSA tree, these are all separate operands of width `SUM_WIDTH+1`, and they overlap completely (same bit positions). So the correction should be `popcount(pipe3_signs)` at bit 0.

The `$unsigned(pipe3_signs)` is 17 bits. If I add it to `csa_sum + csa_carry` at the LSB, that gives `popcount` value. This should be correct. Let me check if the issue is the zero-extension. When `signs[y]=0`, we use `mantissa` directly. When `signs[y]=1`, we use `~mantissa` (which is `~product_shifted` zero-extended). The original was `~mantissa + 1`. The correction adds `1` for each `signs[y]=1`, so total correction = `popcount(signs)` at bit 0. This should be equivalent.

Actually wait - the issue is that in the original, `mantissa` is `product_shifted` (ALIGN_WIDTH bits) zero-extended to `SUM_WIDTH+1` bits. The `~mantissa` is the ones' complement of the full `SUM_WIDTH+1` bits. The `+1` makes it two's complement. My correction adds `popcount(signs)` at bit 0 of the total sum. That IS correct.

Let me check if maybe the issue is just a simulation race with the `pipe3_signs` register. Actually, let me check if maybe the signs need to be computed differently. Let me revert the ones' complement change and go back to the working version:

Good, back to the correct 893K version. The critical path is still: `pipe2_rg_exp_max` → exp_diff → shift_amount → barrel_shifter → two's_complement → `pipe3_mantissa_comp`.

The path goes through the barrel shifter (big mux chains) and then the XOR/+1 of two's complement. Let me try a different approach: **register the shift amounts** instead of computing them from `pipe2_rg_exp_max`. Move the exponent difference computation to pipe1→pipe2, and register the shift amounts.

Looking at the path more carefully: `pipe2_rg_exp_max` and `pipe2_rg_exp_items` are both registered. The exponent diff, shift amount, barrel shift, and complement are all combinational. If I move the exp_diff + shift_amount computation to be registered (i.e., compute them in pipe1→pipe2 and register them), then pipe2→pipe3 only has barrel_shift + complement.

But the issue is that `rg_exp_max` is computed from `pipe1_rg_exp_c` and `pipe1_rg_exp_acc` (both registered in pipe1). So `rg_exp_max` is combinational from pipe1 registers. The exp diff needs `rg_exp_max` and `rg_exp_items` (also combinational from pipe1). So I can compute exp_diff + shift_amount in the pipe1→pipe2 stage and register them.

ALIGN_WIDTH : rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: SHIFT_WIDTH];\n            end\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin\n                assign shift_amount_pre[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: SHIFT_WIDTH];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate', old_string='    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(\n        .N(N+1),\n        .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)\n    ) u_comp_tree(\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );')

[ASSISTANT]
Same error pattern as before. The problem might be more subtle. The error says `exp=0000 got=0002`, meaning the result should be 0 (NaR/zero case) but we're getting 0x0002. This suggests the correction is being applied even when the result should be zero.

Let me think about this differently. The issue is the `+1` correction. When `mantissa` is all zeros (zero-extended `product_shifted`), the two's complement should also be 0 (since `~0 + 1 = 0` in modular arithmetic with enough bits). But with ones' complement, `~0 = all ones`, which when summed gives a large positive number. The correction adds `popcount(signs)`, but `~0 + 1 = 0` means the all-ones value plus 1 should give 0. However, `all-ones` in a `SUM_WIDTH+1` bit field is `2^(SUM_WIDTH+1) - 1`, and adding 1 gives `2^(SUM_WIDTH+1)` which wraps to 0 in `SUM_WIDTH+1` bits. So the ones' complement approach should work for a single lane.

But the issue is that when multiple lanes are negated, the correction is `popcount(signs)` at bit 0. If all 17 lanes are 0 and all 17 are negated, we get `17 * (2^(SUM_WIDTH+1) - 1) + 17 = 17 * 2^(SUM_WIDTH+1)` which wraps to 0 mod `2^(SUM_WIDTH+1)`. That should work.

Wait, the issue might be that the original mantissa_comp uses `+1` per-lane, and the carry can propagate across the full width. The CSA tree sums the `mantissa_comp` values. The final sum is `csa_sum + csa_carry`. My correction adds `popcount(signs)` to this. But the CSA tree's carry output is left-shifted by 1, so the `+1` at bit 0 of each lane would be handled differently in the CSA.

Actually, I think the issue is that each lane's `+1` is at bit 0 of that lane's `(SUM_WIDTH+1)`-bit field. But in the CSA tree, all lanes overlap at the same bit positions (they're all `(SUM_WIDTH+1)`-bit numbers being added together). So the `+1` for each lane is at bit 0 of the total sum. Adding `popcount(signs)` at bit 0 is correct. BUT the CSA tree carries might handle this differently...

Wait, I realize the problem might be a different subtlety. The original `mantissa_comp` for a negated lane is `~mantissa + 1` in `SUM_WIDTH+1` bits. My version is `~mantissa` in `SUM_WIDTH+1` bits. The difference is exactly `+1` per negated lane. When the CSA tree adds all `N+1` operands, the result is:
- Original: `sum(~m_i + s_i)` = `sum(~m_i) + sum(s_i)` = `sum(~m_i) + popcount(signs)`
- My version: `sum(~m_i)` for negated, `sum(m_i)` for non-negated = `sum(~m_i * s_i + m_i * (1-s_i))`

These are different! The original computes `sum(~m_i + s_i) = sum(~m_i) + popcount(s)`. My version computes `sum(if s_i then ~m_i else m_i)`. These are NOT the same thing.

The key difference: when `s_i=0` (non-negated), the original uses `m_i` and my version also uses `m_i`. When `s_i=1` (negated), the original uses `~m_i + 1` and my version uses `~m_i`. So the difference is exactly `popcount(signs)`. The final sum should differ by exactly `popcount(signs)`. So adding `popcount(signs)` to the final sum should correct it.

But wait - `popcount(signs)` can be up to 17 (for N=16, N+1=17 lanes). Adding this to `csa_sum + csa_carry` at bit 0 should work. Let me check if the `{{(SUM_WIDTH-N){1'b0}}, pipe3_signs_neg}` is correct. SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH = 14 + 5 = 19. N = 16. So `SUM_WIDTH - N = 19 - 16 = 3`. The extension is 3 zero bits + 17 sign bits = 20 bits. But `sum_result` is `SUM_WIDTH+1 = 20` bits. So the width is correct.

Hmm, maybe the issue is that `pipe3_signs_neg` is registered from `signs_neg = pipe2_signs`, but the `mantissa_comp` is combinational from `pipe2_signs`. Both get registered into pipe3, so they should be aligned. Let me debug by checking if maybe the issue is that I'm not getting the correction right for the zero case.

Actually wait - I bet the issue is that when `mantissa = 0` (all zeros) and `sign = 1`, the two's complement `~0 + 1 = 0` (wraps around), but the ones' complement `~0 = all ones` gives a huge value. Even though the correction should fix it, maybe there's a carry overflow issue. Let me think...

`~0` in 20 bits = `0xFFFFF`. If we have just one negated zero lane: CSA gives `0xFFFFF` + correction `1` = `0x100000` = 0 in 20 bits. OK, that wraps. But the CSA tree operates on `SUM_WIDTH+1 = 20` bit values and produces `sum_o` and `carry_o` both 20 bits. The carry_o is `{1'b0, carry, 1'b0}` shifted... Actually, the CSA tree's carry output is left-shifted by 1 relative to the sum. So `csa_sum + csa_carry` is the true sum. Adding `popcount` to that should be fine.

Actually, let me try reducing the multiplier. The booth multiplier uses radix-4 Booth encoding which is complex. For 4-bit mantissas (MANT_WIDTH_I=3, so MANT_WIDTH_I+1=4), the product is 8 bits. A simple `*` operator might be simpler and let Yosys optimize better.

## Summary

### Final Result
- **Best cost:** 800,573 area_delay_product (down from 968,045 baseline = 17.2% reduction)
- **Best file:** `design.v` (the version before the last split-CSA regression)
- **Final design PASSes 100% of 1,999,992 test vectors**

### What Worked Best

1. **Moving mantissa sum+carry and fixed-shift product to pipe1→pipe2** (pre-computing `mants_norm_c` and `product` before the pipe2 register): Broke the original critical path through exp_max→exp_diff→shift→barrel_shift→complement. Saved ~44ps delay and ~34 area. Cost: 968K → 893K.

2. **Replacing radix-4 Booth multiplier with simple `*` operator**: The 4-bit×4-bit mantissa multiply was over-engineered with Booth encoding + CSA tree. A direct `a * b` let Yosys optimize better, reducing both area and delay significantly. Cost: 861K → 819K.

3. **Ones' complement with popcount correction**: Replaced per-lane two's complement `~x+1` (incrementer on critical path) with `~x` (ones' complement) + a registered popcount of signs added to the final sum. Removed the ripple incrementer from the barrel-shifter→pipe3 path. Cost: 893K → 861K.

4. **Simplifying the comparator module** to a single `>=` comparison instead of the sign/magnitude case statement. Cost: 819K → 804K.

5. **Injecting popcount as 18th CSA operand** instead of adding it to the final sum separately. Cost: 804K → 800K.

### What Didn't Work / Regressions

1. **Ones' complement with simple `$unsigned(signs)` addition**: First attempt failed correctness (1.7M errors) — the popcount correction wasn't being computed properly. Required an explicit popcount tree of adders to work.

2. **Pre-computing shift amounts in pipe1→pipe2**: Moving the exp_diff→shift_amount computation earlier overloaded the pipe1→pipe2 stage (comparator tree + exp diff + shift amount = 1075ps), making it worse than the original.

3. **Direct `>>` shift instead of barrel_shifter module**: Yosys didn't optimize the inline shift as well as the explicit barrel shifter (915K vs 893K).

4. **Splitting CSA and final add into separate stages** (last attempt): Moving the final addition to pipe4→pipe5 overloaded that stage with final_add + mantissa_norm + posit_encode, creating a 951ps critical path. Cost regressed to 918K.

### Lessons Learned

- **Simple operators often beat hand-crafted structures** for small widths — Yosys's technology mapping for `*` and `>=` is well-optimized, while custom Booth multipliers and sign/magnitude comparators add overhead.
- **Pipeline retiming is the primary lever** for delay reduction — moving logic between stages to balance critical paths is more impactful than micro-optimizing individual modules.
- **Ones' complement trick** (deferring the `+1` as a popcount correction) is a classic and effective technique to remove incrementers from critical paths, but requires careful popcount computation.
- **Stage splitting must consider total load** — splitting one stage can overload the next; the whole pipeline must be rebalanced.
- **Always save working variants** before trying risky changes, and evaluate after every edit.
